The inclusion of \u003cstrong\u003eMelatonin\u003c\/strong\u003e allows for \u003cem\u003ein vitro\u003c\/em\u003e modeling of circadian rhythm regulation and potent free-radical scavenging. class=\"section-heading\"\u003eProduct Details\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"space-y-4 text-gray-700\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDescription:\u003c\/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003eDelta Sleep-Inducing Peptide (DSIP) is a naturally occurring, amphiphilic nonapeptide first isolated from the venous blood of rabbits induced into a state of slow-wave sleep. While originally named for its prominent effects on sleep architecture, DSIP is recognized as a broad-spectrum neuromodulator with diverse regulatory functions across the central nervous system and peripheral tissues.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eIn an \u003cem\u003ein vitro\u003c\/em\u003e and laboratory research context, DSIP is extensively studied for its neuroendocrine signaling properties. Researchers utilize DSIP to investigate its modulatory influence on the release of key hormones, including corticotropin, luteinizing hormone (LH), and somatostatin. Furthermore, experimental models explore its role in stress-induced metabolic pathways, antioxidant activity, and the regulation of circadian rhythms at the cellular level.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003c\/div\u003e\n\u003c\/section\u003e\n\u003c!-- Intended Use Section --\u003e\n\u003csection\u003e\n\u003ch3 class=\"section-heading\"\u003eIntended Use\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"content-list text-gray-700\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is sold strictly for \u003cstrong\u003ein vitro laboratory research and development purposes ONLY\u003c\/strong\u003e.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIt is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e intended for human or veterinary use, consumption, diagnostics, therapeutic applications, or any other use outside of controlled laboratory research.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDSIP, as supplied, is an experimental compound intended for scientific investigation into neuromodulation and neuroendocrine signaling.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e an FDA-approved drug, dietary supplement, or cosmetic or intended for any clinical use.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003c\/section\u003e\n\u003c!-- Handling and Storage Section --\u003e\n\u003csection\u003e\n\u003ch3 class=\"section-heading\"\u003eHandling and Storage\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"content-list text-gray-700\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eStorage:\u003c\/strong\u003e Store lyophilized product at -20°C to -80°C. class=\"section-heading\"\u003eProduct Details\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"space-y-4 text-gray-700\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDescription:\u003c\/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003eRT-GLP3 is a synthetic peptide and a novel multi-receptor agonist. It is a single-molecule polypeptide engineered for stability and activity at three different receptors involved in metabolic regulation: the glucagon-like peptide-1 (GLP-1), glucose-dependent insulinotropic polypeptide (GIP), and glucagon receptors.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eIn a research context, Retatrutide is a subject of intense interest due to its tri-agonist mechanism. It is used as a tool in advanced metabolic research to investigate the complex and potentially synergistic effects of combined GLP-1, GIP, and glucagon receptor agonism in various controlled laboratory settings and cell culture models.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003c\/div\u003e\n\u003c\/section\u003e\n\u003c!-- Intended Use Section --\u003e\n\u003csection\u003e\n\u003ch3 class=\"section-heading\"\u003eIntended Use\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"content-list text-gray-700\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is sold strictly for \u003cstrong\u003ein vitro laboratory research and development purposes ONLY\u003c\/strong\u003e.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIt is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e intended for human or veterinary use, consumption, diagnostics, therapeutic applications, or any other use outside of controlled laboratory research.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRetatrutide, as supplied, is an experimental compound intended for scientific investigation into the complex mechanisms of combined GLP-1, GIP, and glucagon receptor agonism in controlled laboratory settings.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e an FDA-approved drug, dietary supplement, or cosmetic or intended for any clinical use.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003c\/section\u003e\n\u003c!-- Handling, Delivery, and Storage Section --\u003e\n\u003csection\u003e\n\u003ch3 class=\"section-heading\"\u003eHandling and Storage\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"content-list text-gray-700\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eStorage:\u003c\/strong\u003e Store lyophilized product at −20°C to −80°C. Protect from moisture and light. It is a 32-amino acid polypeptide that has been structurally modified for increased stability and a prolonged half-life compared to its native counterpart. \u003cbr\u003e\u003cbr\u003eIn a research context, Cagrilintide serves as a potent agonist for the amylin receptor. It is a valuable tool for \u003cem\u003ein vitro\u003c\/em\u003e studies investigating the cellular and metabolic pathways regulated by amylin. Researchers often use this compound, sometimes in conjunction with GLP-1 receptor agonists, to explore the synergistic effects on signaling cascades related to metabolic control in various cell culture models.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eIntended Use\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is sold strictly for \u003cstrong\u003ein vitro laboratory research and development purposes ONLY\u003c\/strong\u003e.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIt is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e intended for human or veterinary use, consumption, diagnostics, therapeutic applications, or any other use outside of controlled laboratory research.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCagrilintide, as supplied, is an experimental compound intended for scientific investigation into the amylin receptor signaling pathway.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e an FDA-approved drug, dietary supplement, or cosmetic or intended for any clinical use.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eHandling and Storage\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eStorage:\u003c\/strong\u003e Store lyophilized product at −20°C to −80°C. This product is supplied as a sterile, concentrated solution, recognized for its distinct chemical structure and high water solubility. \u003cbr\u003e\u003cbr\u003eIn a research context, L-Carnitine is a critical tool for studying cellular energy metabolism. Its primary investigated function is its role in the transport of long-chain fatty acids from the cytosol into the mitochondrial matrix for subsequent β-oxidation. It is widely used in \u003cem\u003ein vitro\u003c\/em\u003e models to explore cellular respiration, fatty acid metabolism, and the carnitine shuttle system.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003c\/div\u003e\n\u003c\/section\u003e\n\u003c!-- Intended Use Section --\u003e\n\u003csection\u003e\n\u003ch3 class=\"section-heading\"\u003eIntended Use\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"content-list text-gray-700\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is sold strictly for \u003cstrong\u003ein vitro laboratory research and development purposes ONLY\u003c\/strong\u003e.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIt is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e intended for human or veterinary use, consumption, diagnostics, therapeutic applications, or any other use outside of controlled laboratory research.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eL-Carnitine, as supplied, is an experimental compound intended for scientific investigation into cellular energy metabolism.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e an FDA-approved drug, dietary supplement, or cosmetic or intended for any clinical use.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003c\/section\u003e\n\u003c!-- Handling and Storage Section --\u003e\n\u003csection\u003e\n\u003ch3 class=\"section-heading\"\u003eHandling and Storage\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"content-list text-gray-700\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eStorage:\u003c\/strong\u003e Store the sterile solution at 2°C to 8°C (standard refrigerator temperature). This comprehensive formulation contains a precise balance of vital lipotropic agents (Methionine, Inositol, Choline) alongside high-dose L-Carnitine and essential B-complex coenzymes.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eIn a laboratory setting, this solution supplies the primary biochemical transport mechanisms necessary for cellular beta-oxidation. \u003cstrong\u003eL-Carnitine\u003c\/strong\u003e facilitates the translocation of long-chain fatty acids into the mitochondrial matrix for ATP production. The synergistic inclusion of the \u003cstrong\u003eMIC blend (Methionine, Inositol, Choline)\u003c\/strong\u003e provides a robust foundation for investigating hepatic lipid export and cellular membrane integrity. Furthermore, \u003cstrong\u003eNADH\u003c\/strong\u003e and \u003cstrong\u003eB-Vitamins (B6, B12)\u003c\/strong\u003e serve as critical electron carriers and enzymatic cofactors, enabling advanced \u003cem\u003ein vitro\u003c\/em\u003e studies of cellular respiration and bioenergetic capacity.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003c\/div\u003e\n\u003c\/section\u003e\n\u003c!-- Intended Use Section --\u003e\n\u003csection\u003e\n\u003ch3 class=\"section-heading\"\u003eIntended Use\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"content-list text-gray-700\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is intended solely for \u003cstrong\u003ein vitro studies\u003c\/strong\u003e, cell culture supplementation, and biochemical assay development.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIt is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e intended for human or veterinary use, consumption, diagnostics, therapeutic applications, or any other use outside of controlled laboratory research.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e an FDA-approved drug, dietary supplement, or cosmetic or intended for any clinical use.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003c\/section\u003e\n\u003c!-- Handling, Delivery, and Storage Section --\u003e\n\u003csection\u003e\n\u003ch3 class=\"section-heading\"\u003eHandling and Storage\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"content-list text-gray-700\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eStorage:\u003c\/strong\u003e Store the sterile solution refrigerated at 2°C to 8°C. Keep the vial tightly sealed to maintain sterility. Protect from direct light. \u003cstrong\u003eDo not freeze\u003c\/strong\u003e, as freezing may compromise the structural integrity of the complex.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eHandling:\u003c\/strong\u003e The solution is pre-mixed and ready for dilution or direct application in laboratory models. Aseptic technique is required during extraction to maintain the sterility of the remaining contents.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eSafety:\u003c\/strong\u003e Handle using appropriate personal protective equipment (PPE) such as gloves, lab coat, and eye protection, within a controlled laboratory environment.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003c\/section\u003e\n\u003c!-- Quality Assurance Section --\u003e\n\u003csection\u003e\n\u003ch3 class=\"section-heading\"\u003eQuality Assurance\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"content-list text-gray-700\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAll active amino acids, lipotropic agents, and vitamins within LIPO-C + are synthesized to high purity specifications prior to solution compounding.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe final solution is sterile-filtered and contains a verified preservative\/buffer system to ensure suitability and stability for sensitive \u003cem\u003ein vitro\u003c\/em\u003e cellular models.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003c\/section\u003e\n\u003c!-- Disclaimer Section --\u003e\n\u003csection\u003e\n\u003ch3 class=\"section-heading\"\u003eDisclaimer\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cblockquote class=\"disclaimer-quote\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003ePurchase of this product signifies an agreement that it will be used solely for legitimate laboratory research purposes by qualified individuals trained in laboratory procedures and safety. This comprehensive formulation contains a precise balance of vital B-complex vitamins alongside powerful lipotropic agents (Choline and Inositol). It is specifically designed to provide optimal nutritional scaffolding for cell culture media and biochemical assays focused on dermal, follicular, and metabolic pathways.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eIn a laboratory setting, this solution supplies essential coenzymes necessary for cellular metabolism, energy production (ATP synthesis), and DNA repair. This blend is provided in a single vial for streamlined laboratory research.\u003cbr\u003e\u003cbr\u003eIn a research context, this complex is intended for \u003cem\u003ein vitro\u003c\/em\u003e studies focusing on the growth hormone (GH) axis. The combination of a GHRH analogue and a GHRP allows for the investigation of synergistic effects on pituitary somatotrophs. Research applications include exploring pulsatile GH release patterns and studying the distinct signaling pathways (the GHRH receptor and the ghrelin receptor\/GH secretagogue receptor) involved in GH secretion in cell culture models.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eIntended Use\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is sold strictly for \u003cstrong\u003ein vitro laboratory research and development purposes ONLY\u003c\/strong\u003e.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIt is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e intended for human or veterinary use, consumption, diagnostics, therapeutic applications, or any other use outside of controlled laboratory research.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis peptide complex, as supplied, is an experimental product for investigating the combined effects of a GHRH analogue and a GHRP on pituitary cell function in a controlled laboratory setting.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e an FDA-approved drug, dietary supplement, or cosmetic or intended for any clinical use.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eHandling and Storage\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eStorage:\u003c\/strong\u003e Store lyophilized product at −20°C to −80°C. This product is supplied as a sterile, concentrated solution, recognized for its distinct chemical structure and high water solubility. \u003cbr\u003e\u003cbr\u003eIn a research context, L-Carnitine is a critical tool for studying cellular energy metabolism. Its primary investigated function is its role in the transport of long-chain fatty acids from the cytosol into the mitochondrial matrix for subsequent β-oxidation. height: 22.7337px;\"\u003eC\u003csub\u003e10\u003c\/sub\u003eH\u003csub\u003e17\u003c\/sub\u003eN\u003csub\u003e3\u003c\/sub\u003eO\u003csub\u003e6\u003c\/sub\u003eS\u003c\/td\u003e\n\u003c\/tr\u003e\n\u003c\/tbody\u003e\n\u003c\/table\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eProduct Details\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eL-Glutathione is a tripeptide composed of the amino acids glutamic acid, cysteine, and glycine. It is a major endogenous antioxidant naturally produced by cells and plays a fundamental role in maintaining cellular homeostasis.\u003cbr\u003e\u003cbr\u003eIn a research context, L-Glutathione is a critical tool for investigating cellular defense mechanisms against oxidative stress. It is widely used in *in vitro* models to study detoxification pathways, redox signaling, and the cellular response to toxins and free radicals. Its role as a primary antioxidant makes it a subject of extensive research in cellular biology and toxicology.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eL-Glutathione vs. Reduced Glutathione\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eIn scientific literature and laboratory contexts, the terms \"L-Glutathione\" and \"Reduced Glutathione (GSH)\" are often used interchangeably. Both refer to the same molecule in its active, antioxidant state. The \"L\" denotes the stereoisomer of the glutamic acid residue, which is the biologically active form. \"Reduced\" refers to the state of the thiol group on the cysteine residue, which is capable of donating an electron to neutralize free radicals. The product supplied is the stable, reduced form of L-Glutathione, suitable for all standard research applications investigating its antioxidant properties.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eIntended Use\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is sold strictly for \u003cstrong\u003ein vitro laboratory research and development purposes ONLY\u003c\/strong\u003e.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIt is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e intended for human or veterinary use, consumption, diagnostics, therapeutic applications, or any other use outside of controlled laboratory research.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eL-Glutathione, as supplied, is an experimental compound intended for scientific investigation into the mechanisms of oxidative stress, cellular detoxification, and redox signaling in controlled laboratory settings.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e an FDA-approved drug, dietary supplement, or cosmetic or intended for any clinical use.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eHandling and Storage\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eStorage:\u003c\/strong\u003e Store lyophilized product at −20°C to −80°C. Details\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDescription:\u003c\/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003eOxytocin is a cyclic nonapeptide with the amino acid sequence Cys-Tyr-Ile-Gln-Asn-Cys-Pro-Leu-Gly-NH₂. It functions as a peptide hormone and neuropeptide in mammals, distinguished by its cyclic structure formed by a disulfide bridge between its two cysteine residues. \u003cbr\u003e\u003cbr\u003eIn a research context, Oxytocin is a primary tool for investigating the oxytocin receptor (OXTR) signaling pathway, which is a G protein-coupled receptor. Preclinical studies often utilize this compound to explore its downstream effects on cellular behavior, such as calcium signaling and gene expression, in various \u003cem\u003ein vitro\u003c\/em\u003e models.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eIntended Use\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is sold strictly for \u003cstrong\u003ein vitro laboratory research and development purposes ONLY\u003c\/strong\u003e.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIt is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e intended for human or veterinary use, consumption, diagnostics, therapeutic applications, or any other use outside of controlled laboratory research.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOxytocin, as supplied here, is an experimental tool for scientific investigation into its mechanisms of action in controlled laboratory settings.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e an FDA-approved drug or for any clinical use.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eHandling and Storage\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eStorage:\u003c\/strong\u003e Store lyophilized product at −20°C to −80°C. Details\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDescription:\u003c\/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003eNicotinamide Adenine Dinucleotide (NAD+) is a dinucleotide and a crucial coenzyme found in all living cells. It is composed of two nucleotides joined through their phosphate groups: one nucleotide contains an adenine nucleobase and the other nicotinamide. NAD+ exists in two forms, an oxidized form (NAD+) and a reduced form (NADH), which are central to its function. \u003cbr\u003e\u003cbr\u003eIn a research context, NAD+ is fundamental for studying numerous biological processes. It serves as a key electron carrier in redox reactions, making it essential for investigating cellular respiration and energy metabolism (e.g., glycolysis, Krebs cycle). It is also a critical substrate for enzymes involved in cell signaling and DNA repair, such as sirtuins and PARPs, making it a vital compound for in vitro studies in molecular biology and biochemistry.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eIntended Use\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product, Nicotinamide Adenine Dinucleotide (NAD+), is sold exclusively for \u003cstrong\u003ein vitro laboratory research and development purposes ONLY.\u003c\/strong\u003e\n\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIt is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e intended for human or veterinary use, consumption, diagnostics, therapeutic applications, or any other use outside of controlled laboratory research environments.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is intended for researchers seeking to further explore the roles and functions of NAD+ in various biological systems and experimental models.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e an FDA-approved drug, dietary supplement, or cosmetic.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eHandling and Storage\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eStorage:\u003c\/strong\u003e Store lyophilized product at −20°C to −80°C. Details\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDescription:\u003c\/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003eSS-31, also known as Elamipretide, is a synthetic, water-soluble tetrapeptide. It is an aromatic-cationic peptide, a class of molecules characterized by alternating aromatic and cationic amino acid residues, which allows it to readily penetrate cell membranes. \u003cbr\u003e\u003cbr\u003eIn a research context, SS-31 is investigated as a mitochondria-targeting antioxidant. Its unique structure enables it to selectively concentrate in the inner mitochondrial membrane, independent of membrane potential. It is used as a tool in preclinical studies to explore its role in scavenging reactive oxygen species (ROS), interacting with cardiolipin, and protecting mitochondrial function against oxidative stress in various in vitro models.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eIntended Use\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is sold strictly for in vitro laboratory research and development purposes ONLY.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIt is NOT intended for human or veterinary use, consumption, diagnostics, therapeutic applications, or any other use outside of controlled laboratory research.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSS-31, as supplied, is an experimental compound intended for scientific investigation into mitochondrial function, oxidative stress, and related cellular pathways in controlled laboratory settings.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is NOT an FDA-approved drug, dietary supplement, or cosmetic or intended for any clinical use.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eHandling and Storage\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eStorage:\u003c\/strong\u003e Store lyophilized product at −20°C to −80°C. Protect from moisture and light. efficient separation of components from biological samples, such as whole blood. Each 15 mL tube contains ACD-A solution to act as an anticoagulant and an inert thixotropic gel separator. \u003cbr\u003e\u003cbr\u003eIn a research context, these tubes are used for density gradient centrifugation. During centrifugation, the gel migrates to form a stable physical barrier between the plasma\/platelet layer and the erythrocyte layer. This allows for the easy and high-quality separation of sample components for subsequent *in vitro* analysis, cell culture, or other downstream research applications.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eIntended Use\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is sold strictly for in vitro laboratory research and development purposes ONLY.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIt is intended for use as a consumable for the separation of biological samples in a controlled laboratory environment.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis is a lab supply product and is NOT intended for human or veterinary use, therapeutic applications, or any clinical procedures.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eHandling and Storage\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eStorage:\u003c\/strong\u003e Store tubes at controlled room temperature. It contains 0.9% (9 mg\/mL) of benzyl alcohol, which acts as a bacteriostatic preservative by inhibiting the growth of most contaminating bacteria. \u003cbr\u003e\u003cbr\u003eIn a research context, this solution is essential for the reconstitution of lyophilized (freeze-dried) compounds, such as peptides, for use in *in vitro* experiments. The inclusion of a bacteriostatic agent allows for repeated withdrawals from a single vial while maintaining the sterility and integrity of the reconstituted research solution for approximately 28 days.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eIntended Use\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is sold strictly for in vitro laboratory research and development purposes ONLY.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIt is NOT intended for human or veterinary use, consumption, diagnostics, or therapeutic applications.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis solution is intended solely for use as a sterile solvent for the dilution or reconstitution of research compounds for laboratory analysis.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is NOT an FDA-approved drug, dietary supplement, or cosmetic or intended for any clinical use.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eHandling and Storage\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eStorage:\u003c\/strong\u003e Store unopened vials at controlled room temperature, below 30°C (86°F). Protect from light and keep in a dry place.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eAfter Opening:\u003c\/strong\u003e Once a vial is opened, the benzyl alcohol preservative remains effective for approximately 28 days. After this period, the vial should be discarded to prevent potential contamination.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eSafety:\u003c\/strong\u003e Handle using aseptic techniques in a clean or sterile environment to prevent contamination of the vial's contents.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eQuality Assurance\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOur Bacteriostatic Water is prepared to USP specifications for sterility and pyrogenicity.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eEach batch is tested to ensure the correct concentration of benzyl alcohol and the absence of contaminants.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eFurther Information and Verification\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eBacteriostatic Water is a preparation and does not have a unique CAS number. Pink\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eProduct Details\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDescription:\u003c\/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003eThe Padded Storage Case is a professional solution for organizing and protecting your valuable research samples. This durable case, available in multiple colors, features a soft, precision-cut padded foam insert designed to securely hold up to seven (7) vials (up to 3mL in size). \u003cbr\u003e\u003cbr\u003eIn a research context, this compact and convenient case is essential for maintaining sample integrity. It prevents vial breakage during transport and storage, minimizes the risk of cross-contamination, and keeps your research compounds neatly organized. The lightweight design makes it an ideal accessory for any laboratory environment where the protection of delicate samples is paramount.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eIntended Use\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is sold strictly for in vitro laboratory research and development purposes ONLY.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIt is intended to be used as a protective storage and transport case for research vials in a controlled laboratory environment.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis is a lab supply product and is NOT intended for human or veterinary use, or for any application outside of a research setting.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eHandling and Storage\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eStorage:\u003c\/strong\u003e Store in a clean, dry place at controlled room temperature.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eUsage:\u003c\/strong\u003e Ensure vials are properly sealed before placing them in the case to prevent leaks.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eQuality Assurance\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOur storage cases are manufactured from high-quality, durable materials suitable for repeated laboratory use.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eDisclaimer\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp\u003ePurchase of this product signifies an agreement that it will be used solely for legitimate laboratory research purposes by qualified individuals trained in laboratory procedures and safety. The buyer assumes all responsibility for the proper handling, storage, use, and disposal of this product. The seller makes no claims regarding the suitability of this product for any particular research application. Details\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDescription:\u003c\/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003eIGF-1 LR3 (Long Arginine 3-IGF-1) is a synthetic, recombinant protein analogue of Insulin-like Growth Factor-1 (IGF-1). This analogue is structurally modified with an N-terminal extension of 13 amino acids and a substitution of an arginine for the glutamic acid at the third position. These modifications significantly reduce its binding affinity to IGF-binding proteins (IGFBPs). \u003cbr\u003e\u003cbr\u003eIn a research context, the reduced affinity for IGFBPs makes IGF-1 LR3 a potent tool for *in vitro* studies. It is used to investigate cellular growth, proliferation, and differentiation pathways by more effectively stimulating the IGF-1 receptor (IGF-1R). Researchers utilize this compound to explore its effects on cellular signaling cascades, such as the PI3K\/Akt pathway, in various cell culture models.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eIntended Use\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is sold strictly for \u003cstrong\u003ein vitro laboratory research and development purposes ONLY\u003c\/strong\u003e.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIt is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e intended for human or veterinary use, consumption, diagnostics, therapeutic applications, or any other use outside of controlled laboratory research.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIGF-1 LR3, as supplied, is an experimental compound intended for scientific investigation into the mechanisms of the IGF-1 receptor signaling pathway in controlled laboratory settings.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e an FDA-approved drug, dietary supplement, or cosmetic or intended for any clinical use.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eHandling and Storage\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eStorage:\u003c\/strong\u003e Store lyophilized product at −20°C to −80°C. Protect from moisture and light. Details\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDescription:\u003c\/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003eCJC-1295 no DAC, also known as Modified GRF (1-29), is a synthetic analogue of Growth Hormone Releasing Hormone (GHRH). It is a truncated peptide containing the first 29 amino acids of GHRH, which are responsible for its biological activity. This version is further modified with four amino acid substitutions at positions 2, 8, 15, and 27 to increase its stability and prevent rapid degradation. \u003cbr\u003e\u003cbr\u003eIn a research context, CJC-1295 no DAC is used as a tool to investigate the GHRH receptor (GHRHR) and its associated signaling pathways. Due to its increased resistance to enzymatic cleavage compared to native GHRH, it provides a more stable means of stimulating the GHRHR in *in vitro* models, allowing researchers to study pituitary somatotroph response and the mechanisms of pulsatile growth hormone release in a controlled laboratory environment.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eIntended Use\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is sold strictly for \u003cstrong\u003ein vitro laboratory research and development purposes ONLY\u003c\/strong\u003e.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIt is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e intended for human or veterinary use, consumption, diagnostics, therapeutic applications, or any other use outside of controlled laboratory research.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCJC-1295 no DAC, as supplied, is an experimental compound intended for scientific investigation into the GHRH signaling pathway.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e an FDA-approved drug, dietary supplement, or cosmetic or intended for any clinical use.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eHandling and Storage\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eStorage:\u003c\/strong\u003e Store lyophilized product at −20°C to −80°C. Details\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDescription:\u003c\/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003eEpithalon, also known as Epitalon, is a synthetic tetrapeptide with the amino acid sequence Ala-Glu-Asp-Gly. It was developed as a synthetic analogue of epithalamin, a polypeptide complex extracted from the pineal gland of cattle. \u003cbr\u003e\u003cbr\u003eIn a research context, Epithalon is a subject of significant interest in the study of cellular aging. It is primarily used as a tool in *in vitro* models to investigate its effects on telomerase activity and telomere elongation. Preclinical research often focuses on its potential to modulate gene expression and its influence on the cell cycle in various cell culture systems.\u003c\/p\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eIntended Use\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is sold strictly for \u003cstrong\u003ein vitro laboratory research and development purposes ONLY\u003c\/strong\u003e.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIt is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e intended for human or veterinary use, consumption, diagnostics, therapeutic applications, or any other use outside of controlled laboratory research.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eEpithalon, as supplied, is an experimental compound intended for scientific investigation into the mechanisms of telomerase activity and cellular aging in controlled laboratory settings.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThis product is \u003cstrong\u003eNOT\u003c\/strong\u003e an FDA-approved drug, dietary supplement, or cosmetic or intended for any clinical use.\u003c\/li\u003e\n\u003c\/ul\u003e\n\u003ch3\u003eHandling and Storage\u003c\/h3\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eStorage:\u003c\/strong\u003e Store lyophilized product at −20°C to −80°C.
